This capture precedes every continued model answer. The separately frozen protocol 217b9c6ee846e401960b63f856bf418696837d29408a92e34996b7127a3362cf names the unchanged original study and the exact closed file inventory of its interrupted run. The original study and its failed receipt remain incomplete. Every completed answer is retained independent of its grade. Eighteen complete paired jobs import without launching a model; the next job retains fifty original and forty-five candidate answers and generates only its five unanswered candidate cells. Subsequent jobs retain their original order and paired arm order. No partial aggregate, quality verdict or answer-dependent selection was inspected.

The native executable, Metal library, model artifacts, task sources, graders, settings, resets, margins, alpha, weights and analysis method remain bound to the original protocol. All unchanged imported grader and resource helpers must have the exact original bytes. The continuation binds its current Python executable and source closure before use. Post-exit settling addresses the known observation-cache boundary; the exact cause of the original native/parent discrepancy remains unresolved. It does not enlarge memory or bypass a preflight, and another failed execution stays incomplete with no automatic retry.

The original 17,305.981057501165 active job seconds, all forty-seven attempted model sessions and 12,124,160 allocated receipt bytes count toward the original total limits. The continuation reserves the remaining 5,987,875,840 receipt bytes within the unchanged staging ceiling. Imported outcomes and sessions must be an exact leading prefix and preserve their original timing. Native transcript readiness, reset/call counts, response bytes, artifact and plan identities, lifetime and sampled physical peaks, completion, and the specifically unloaded preflight failure are checked before importing. A trial validation exposed that the old unloaded receipt intentionally has no post-load display-model field; the final validator authenticates its pre-load artifact fields while requiring the display field to remain absent. Job driver logs are included in custody, distinct from job directories.

Final analysis refuses any incomplete campaign before regrading. It then replays every complete task with the original graders and recorded native replies, verifies requests and all outcome fields, and applies the unchanged preselected paired method. Only the sandbox profile digest, which contains a fresh temporary path, and the independently bounded worker footprint are compared as execution custody rather than deterministic grading values. Both are retained in analysis custody. Analysis and prior-job checks stream jobs instead of retaining the complete study in memory. This is instrument acceptance, not a quality result.

Eleven continuation groups pass on each Python runtime, covering unchanged evidence, omitted/extra files, changed helpers and interpreter, answered/loaded failures, transcript counters and memory, whole-job imports, unanswered-only execution, prior time/storage/session accounting, the last allowed session, exhausted time, no retries and complete regrading. Twelve actual-process native-owner groups and eight product-driver groups also pass on both runtimes; thirty-two static entry-point checks confirm registration.

The separately built native startup diagnostic compiles under its bounded single-worker build, preserves identical before/after source inputs, and passes the complete weights-free native catalogue. It is frozen for future product checks, but does not replace the original study executable. Complete engine and Mac CI pass the pack-bound activation commit 16aa3cb83d8eb3a12dfcc7877fcd5ac21a20a884. Complete engine CI also passes the bounded standalone export driver commit 6218c662ac57f37928cf5dae70e01c8b6de18c9f. These foundation checks do not establish physical product outcomes, standalone export, candidate qualification, twenty-token throughput or a release.

Raw protocol, exact source, checks and prior CI follow. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>; original and normalized byte counts and hashes are explicit. Compression is checked by roundtrip. Partial model answers remain only in their original local files under the preserved custody inventory.
